<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微分量子架构搜索(DQAS)是自动化设计量子电路的有前景框架,尤其适用于变分量子优化算法。然而,DQAS在量子硬件上的实际部署受限于优化过程中需要大量的电路测量,导致硬件执行成本高昂。因此,降低测量成本对于DQAS的实际应用至关重要。

Method: 作者针对一大类组合优化问题和常用的旋转门参数化方案,从理论上推导出一种测量成本降低方案。该方案在不改变优化目标的前提下显著减少电路测量次数。方法通过理论分析揭示了梯度计算中的冗余测量,并设计了优化的测量策略。在3-SAT和MaxCut基准问题上进行了实验验证。

Result: 实验结果表明,该方法将梯度测量成本降低了约39%至41%,同时仅引入可忽略不计的经典后处理开销。在3-SAT和MaxCut问题上的基准测试验证了该方案的有效性,显著降低了在量子硬件上执行DQAS的实际成本。

Conclusion: 本研究成功解决了DQAS在量子硬件部署中的关键瓶颈——高测量成本问题。通过理论推导和实验验证,证明了在组合优化问题中可以大幅降低测量需求(约40%),且不影响优化性能,仅增加极小的经典计算开销。这项工作为DQAS的实际硬件应用铺平了道路,使其更具可行性和实用价值。

Abstract: Differentiable quantum architecture search (DQAS) is a promising framework for the automated design of quantum circuits, particularly for variational quantum optimization algorithms. However, its practical deployment on quantum hardware is limited by the large number of circuit measurements required during optimization, making hardware execution costly. In this work, we show that for a broad class of combinatorial optimization problems and commonly used rotational gate parameterizations, the measurement cost of DQAS can be significantly reduced without changing the optimization objective. We derive the proposed measurement reduction scheme theoretically and validate it experimentally on 3-SAT and MaxCut benchmark problems. Our approach reduces the requested gradient measurement cost by about 39 to 41% while introducing only negligible classical post-processing overhead, lowering the practical cost of executing DQAS on quantum hardware.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程量子模拟器在实现哈密顿量时面临耦合类型受限的挑战,许多系统需要超越传统ZZ耦合(Ising哈密顿量)的耦合类型(如XX+YY耦合)。为了在仅支持Ising哈密顿量的硬件平台上模拟更广泛的量子多体系统,需要开发新的编码和模拟方法。

Method: 采用畴壁编码(domain wall encoding)方法,将原本需要XX+YY耦合的一维自旋链问题转换为仅需Ising哈密顿量(ZZ耦合)的问题。在商用Rydberg原子量子设备上实现该方法,模拟具有不同各向异性参数的Heisenberg XXZ模型的时间演化过程。

Conclusion: 本工作通过畴壁编码方法扩展了模拟经典量子多体系统的工具箱,证明了仅使用Ising耦合的模拟平台可以有效模拟需要更复杂耦合类型的物理模型,为在受限硬件条件下实现更广泛的量子模拟应用提供了实用途径。

Abstract: A major application of analog quantum hardware is quantum simulation, where a system evolves over time according to a given Hamiltonian. One challenge when implementing the Hamiltonian on a programmable quantum simulator is the availability of distinct coupling types, many of which go beyond the conventional ZZ-couplings found in Ising Hamiltonians that are naturally implemented by various platforms. In prior work, we have developed a method based on domain wall encoding to simulate one-dimensional spin chains using only Ising Hamiltonians, which would otherwise require XX+YY-couplings as well. Here, we implement the method on a commercially accessible Rydberg atom quantum device, and simulate the time evolution of the Heisenberg XXZ model with various anisotropies. We successfully probe the XY and Ising phases of the model and reproduce the qualitative behavior of several one- and two-body observables in both phases, as well as at the critical point. Our work augments the toolbox to simulate canonical quantum many-body systems on analog quantum simulators.

</details>


### [97] [CV-QKD with noisy coherent states and realistic displacement receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10414)
*L. F. Melo,J. J. Lacerda,John A. Mora Rodríguez,Alexandre B. Tacla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于位移接收器的连续变量量子密钥分发(CV-QKD)协议,使用二进制噪声相干态字母表,证明了该方案在特定实验条件下可以优于传统零差探测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子态辨别可以通过位移操作和光子探测实现近最优性能,这种位移接收器具有可调控参数的优势。然而,将这种辨别策略转化为完整的CV-QKD协议面临挑战:标准的正交分量测量方法不适用,需要开发新的信道参数估计策略,并且需要解决有限探测器分辨率带来的安全漏洞问题。

Method: 1) 构建基于二进制位移热态字母表和位移接收器的CV-QKD协议;2) 开发使用相同位移接收器进行信道参数估计的策略,替代标准正交分量方法;3) 使用光子数分辨探测器进行可靠的参数估计,分析有限探测器分辨率对噪声估计的影响;4) 针对纠缠克隆集体攻击分析协议在渐近regime下的安全性,涵盖直接和反向协调两种情况;5) 使用最小化辨别错误概率的位移参数作为密钥生成的调谐策略;6) 在不同传输率和噪声参数范围内评估协议性能并与传统零差探测比较。

Result: 1) 协议能够在噪声态制备和非理想探测条件下生成密钥;2) 光子数分辨探测需要足够的分辨率才能实现可靠的参数估计,而开关探测可用于密钥映射;3) 有限探测器分辨率会低估观测噪声并构成潜在安全漏洞,但该漏洞可通过适度的分辨率得到缓解;4) 最小化辨别错误概率的位移参数同样适用于密钥生成,提供了解析可处理的调谐策略;5) 在实验相关的参数范围内,位移接收器在特定传输率和噪声条件下的性能优于传统零差探测。

Conclusion: 位移接收器为CV-QKD实现提供了一种有前景的替代方案。该方案通过二进制噪声相干态和位移探测实现了完整的量子密钥分发协议,具有抗集体攻击的安全性,并在特定实验条件下表现出优于传统零差探测的性能。研究还指出了探测器分辨率对安全性的关键影响,并提供了实用的参数优化策略,为CV-QKD的实际部署提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Near-optimal binary coherent state discrimination can be achieved by performing a displacement operation followed by photon detection. This displacement serves as a control parameter that can be tuned to optimize a chosen figure of merit, which makes such receivers attractive beyond state discrimination. Here, we turn this discrimination strategy into a complete continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CV-QKD) protocol based on a binary alphabet of noisy coherent states (displaced thermal states) and displacement receivers. We develop a strategy for estimating the channel parameters with the same displacement receiver, since standard quadrature-based methods do not apply. While a key map can be obtained with on/off detection, we demonstrate that reliable parameter estimation requires photon-number-resolving detection with sufficient resolution. Finite detector resolution underestimates the observed noise and constitutes a potential security loophole, which we quantify and show to be mitigable with modest resolution. We analyze the protocol's security against entangling-cloner collective attacks in the asymptotic regime, for both direct and reverse reconciliation, and show that it can generate secret keys even under noisy state preparation and imperfect detection. Interestingly, we find that the displacement that minimizes the discrimination error probability also performs well for key generation, thus providing a useful and analytically tractable tuning strategy for the CV-QKD receiver. We evaluate the protocol's performance over a range of transmittances and noise parameters, showing that the displacement receiver can outperform conventional homodyne detection in experimentally relevant regimes. Together, our results point to displacement receivers as a promising alternative for CV-QKD implementations.

</details>


### [98] [Design-Time Conformance Checking for Pulse-Level Quantum Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10427)
*Rylan Malarchick*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了qconform，一个脉冲级量子控制程序的可实现性检查器，用于在编译前验证程序是否符合目标设备的硬件能力约束，避免程序被静默修改或运行时失败导致实验数据与预期不符的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲级量子控制程序在超出设备限制时可能被编译器拒绝、静默修改或运行时失败，后两种情况会导致实验数据与编写的程序不对应。现有工具链缺乏设计阶段的、确定性的、离线的可实现性验证机制，硬件能力限制分散在供应商文档和源代码中，难以系统化检查。

Method: 开发qconform检查器，接受脉冲程序和版本化的设备能力描述符作为输入。描述符中的每个约束都引用了建立该约束的工具链观察结果。检查器采用设计时、离线、确定性的方式工作，不使用浮点运算。输出包括判定结果、应用的规则以及覆盖清单（列出未检查的内容）。通过针对QICK和Qblox工具链进行差分测试来评估，测试对象包括三种QICK板配置和一个Qblox集群，共运行1263次程序测试（969个不同程序）。

Result: 在1263次程序运行的差分测试中，检查器没有接受任何被工具链拒绝的程序，显示出高准确性。固定到特定工具链版本的描述符能够接受旧版本拒绝的程序，验证了版本化能力描述的有效性。评估过程中发现检查器及其描述符中的10个缺陷，均已修复。

Conclusion: qconform提供了一种可靠的设计时验证方法，能够在脉冲级量子控制程序编译前确定其在目标设备上的可实现性，避免了静默修改和运行时失败问题。通过版本化的能力描述符，系统能够适应不同工具链版本的能力差异。差分测试验证了检查器的有效性和准确性，未出现误报（接受应被拒绝的程序）。该工具为量子实验提供了更可靠的程序验证机制。

Abstract: A pulse-level quantum control program is written against a device whose limits are recorded, if at all, in vendor documentation and source code. A program that exceeds them can be refused at compile time. It can also be accepted and silently altered, or compile and then fail at the board. In the last two cases the experiment runs, and the data does not correspond to the program that was written. We present qconform, a checker that decides whether a pulse program is realizable on a device, given a versioned capability descriptor for that device. Every constraint in a descriptor cites the toolchain observation that established it. The checker is design-time, offline, and deterministic, and it uses no floating point. It reports a verdict, the rules it applied, and a coverage manifest that names what it did not check. We evaluate qconform by differential testing against the QICK and Qblox toolchains, on three QICK board configurations and one Qblox cluster. Over 1263 program runs of 969 distinct programs, the checker accepted no program that a toolchain refuses. On both vendors, a descriptor pinned to one toolchain release accepts programs that older releases refuse. The evaluation also found ten defects in the checker and its descriptors, all fixed.

</details>


### [99] [Dynamically protected erasure qubit via low-frequency charge driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10432)
*Ahmed Hajr,Ke Wang,Bingcheng Qing,Akel Hashim,Bibek Bhandari,Dominic Briseño-Colunga,Justin Dressel,Andrew N. Jordan,Irfan Siddiqi*

TL;DR: 本文展示了通过亚GHz电荷驱动超导Kerr振荡器实现快速参数化操作与低频噪声抑制的双重优势，并在双轨量子比特上实现了82纳秒快速复位、近四倍擦除偏置以及单次擦除检查后低至1.5×10^-4的Clifford门错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强驱动动力学保护虽能在不完美硬件上实现鲁棒量子处理，但常受驱动诱导的退相和泄漏等寄生过程限制。本研究旨在突破这一瓶颈，探索在超导Kerr振荡器中利用电荷驱动频率依赖性特性，同时激活快速参数化相互作用并抑制低频噪声的方案。

Method: 采用亚GHz频段电荷驱动处于transmon区间的超导Kerr振荡器，利用AC Stark频移作为探测手段，研究振荡器频率对电荷驱动的敏感性与驱动频率及幅度的二次方关系。基于此频率依赖特性，在单个和双Kerr振荡器系统上分别实现快速冷却/复位、双轨量子比特的逻辑控制与动力学保护，并结合电路末端的单次擦除检查进行门操作保真度测试。

Conclusion: 亚GHz电荷驱动超导Kerr振荡器利用其频率敏感性的二次方依赖关系，成功解决了强驱动保护方案中速度与噪声抑制的权衡问题，在快速量子操作、高保真度逻辑门和擦除错误纠正方面展现出显著优势，为容错量子计算提供了有前景的硬件实现路径。

Abstract: Dynamical protection via strong driving can enable resilient quantum processing on imperfect physical hardware. However, the practical utility of such schemes is frequently limited by parasitic processes such as drive-induced dephasing and leakage. Here, we demonstrate that sub-GHz charge driving of superconducting Kerr oscillators (KOs) in the transmon regime circumvents this trade-off, simultaneously activating fast parametric interactions and protecting the encoded qubit from low-frequency noise. The key is the frequency dependence of the charge sensitivity: using the AC Stark shift as a probe, we find that the KO frequency sensitivity to a charge drive grows quadratically with both drive frequency and amplitude, making the oscillator weakly sensitive to $1/f$ charge noise yet strongly coupled to drives near 1~GHz. Exploiting this, we first demonstrate cooling and reset of the KO in 82~ns, to a residual population below $0.7\%$---lower than its $2.5\%$ steady-state thermal population. Next, using two KOs, we demonstrate logical control and dynamical protection of a dual-rail qubit with nearly fourfold erasure bias. Finally, using a single end-of-circuit erasure check, we achieve an error per Clifford of $5.6\times10^{-4}$, which falls to $1.5\times10^{-4}$ after post-selection, with 25~ns gates.

</details>


### [100] [Optimized discrete Wigner representations and non-stabilizerness in qubit systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10471)
*A. B. Klimov,C. Muñoz,I. Sainz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过允许相空间表示根据状态自适应优化，证明了每个量子比特稳定子态都存在精确的δ函数离散Wigner表示，并基于此构造了非稳定子见证量，恢复了量子比特稳定子结构的几何相空间刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为量子比特系统缺乏稳定子特定的相空间描述，这一问题源于使用状态无关的固定离散Wigner映射。为了克服这一局限性，需要发展一种能够适应具体量子态的相空间表示方法，从而为稳定子态提供几何刻画，并建立诊断非稳定子性的有效工具。

Method: 提出了一种优化方法，允许离散Wigner相空间表示根据量子态自适应调整。具体而言：(1)对于任意固定的相空间划分，通过简单的优化程序使每个量子比特稳定子态都能获得精确的δ函数离散Wigner表示；(2)该优化可直接在测量统计层面实现，通过选择构成划分的互不偏基中的最大概率来完成；(3)基于原点处Wigner函数的优化值，自然地构造了一个非稳定子见证量；(4)分析了该见证量在大量子比特极限下的渐近行为和在随机Clifford变换下的不变性。

Result: 证明了每个稳定子态在任意固定相空间划分下都存在精确的δ函数离散Wigner表示。构造的非稳定子见证量表明：稳定子态饱和纯态的通用下界，而严格大于该界的值可认证非稳定子性。尽管该见证量不是有限尺寸的资源单调量，但在大量子比特极限下它变得高度集中，并在随机Clifford变换下有效保持不变，这表明对广泛的非稳定子态族存在渐近线性增长规律。

Conclusion: 本研究恢复了量子比特稳定子结构有意义的几何相空间刻画，打破了稳定子态不存在相空间描述的传统观念。通过状态自适应的优化离散Wigner表示，不仅为稳定子态提供了精确刻画，还建立了可操作的非稳定子性诊断工具。该见证量在大系统极限下的渐近性质为理解量子计算资源的标度行为提供了新视角，对量子优势和容错量子计算的资源理论具有重要意义。

Abstract: The apparent absence of a stabilizer-speci?c phase-space description for qubit systems is not fundamental, but arises from ?xing a state-independent discrete Wigner map. Allowing the phase-space representation to adapt to the state through a simple optimization procedure, we prove that every qubit stabilizer state admits an exact delta-function discrete Wigner representation for any ?xed phase-space partition. This optimization can be implemented directly at the level of measurement statistics by selecting maximal probabilities across mutually unbiased bases forming the partition. This construction naturally leads to a non-stabilizer witness de?ned by the optimized value of the Wigner function at the origin. Stabilizer states saturate the universal lower bound of this witness for pure states, whereas a value strictly larger than the bound certi?es non-stabilizerness. Although the witness is not a ?nite-size resource monotone, it becomes sharply concentrated and e?ectively invariant under random Cli?ord transformations in the large-qubit limit, suggesting an asymptotically linear growth law for broad families of non-stabilizer states. Our results restore a meaningful geometric phase-space characterization of qubit stabilizer structure and provide an operational diagnostic of non-stabilizerness.

</details>


### [101] [Standard estimators cannot represent fault-tolerant workloads at measured error rates: evaluated, evidence-based uncertainty for quantum resource estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10490)
*Furqan Nasir,Sher Jeel Ahmad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开放的、工具无关的框架，用于传播容错量子硬件参数的不确定性，揭示了传统单一数值估计严重低估了量子资源需求，并发现现有主流评估工具在实际测量的错误率下大部分失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法的容错资源估计通常报告为单一数值，但这些估计基于不确定的硬件参数和存在分歧的成本模型。现有的估计方法缺乏对参数不确定性的系统性传播分析，且不同工具间的结构性差异被忽视。需要一个基于证据的、透明的框架来评估量子资源需求的真实不确定性范围，并检验现有工具在实际硬件参数下的适用性。

Method: 构建了一个开放的、工具无关的框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)使用基于证据的先验分布来描述容错硬件参数（如实际测量的错误率中位数约为4×10^-3而非传统假设的10^-3）；(2)将参数不确定性传播到五个表面码成本模型中，包括两个第三方估算器（Azure Quantum Resource Estimator和Qualtran）；(3)加入相关输入敏感性分析，并采用预注册的三层评估协议。将该框架应用于四个典型量子算法任务：RSA-2048、ECC-256、AES-256和Hubbard模型模拟，均基于超导表面码架构。

Result: 产生了三项相互关联的关键发现：(1)传播实际硬件展示的错误率后，物理量子比特数的90%置信区间宽度扩大了约40倍，中位数比传统点估计高出约4.6倍；(2)五个独立构建的成本模型对相同输入产生稳定的2倍差异，这种结构性不确定性无法从单一工具中揭示，但两个第三方估算器彼此间的一致性在10%以内；(3)最重要的是，两个第三方估算器对基于证据的参数空间有80-100%超出其可表示范围，AES-256的门计数甚至直接溢出一个工具的计数器，意味着这些工作负载在实测错误率下根本无法被表示。

Conclusion: 传统的单一数值估计惯例掩盖了量子资源需求评估中的所有关键不确定性。基于证据的参数传播揭示了资源需求的巨大不确定性范围和工具间的结构性分歧。更严重的是，标准评估工具恰好在硬件实际运行的参数区域失效，表明当前主流工具与实际硬件性能之间存在根本性的脱节。这要求量子计算领域采用基于证据、透明化的不确定性评估方法，而非依赖单一的乐观估计值。

Abstract: Estimates of the quantum resources needed to run fault-tolerant algorithms are almost always reported as single numbers, even though they rest on uncertain hardware parameters and on cost models that disagree. We present an open, tool-agnostic framework that propagates evidence-based priors over fault-tolerant hardware parameters through five surface-code cost models, adds correlated-input sensitivity analysis, and evaluates the resulting intervals with a pre-registered three-layer protocol. Applied to RSA-2048, ECC-256, AES-256, and a Hubbard-model simulation on a superconducting surface-code architecture, it produces three coupled findings that together undermine the single-number convention. Propagating the error rates hardware has actually demonstrated, whose median across five large-array devices is about 4 x 10^-3 rather than the conventional 10^-3, widens the 90% physical-qubit interval to roughly forty times and lifts its median about 4.6 times above the conventional point estimate. Against the same inputs, five independently structured cost models, including two third-party estimators (the Azure Quantum Resource Estimator and Qualtran) that agree with each other to within about ten percent, disagree by a stable factor of two, a structural uncertainty that no single tool reveals. Most consequentially, both third-party estimators exceed their representable envelope for 80 to 100 percent of the evidence-based parameter space, and the AES-256 gate count overflows one tool's counter outright, so these workloads cannot be represented at measured error rates at all. The optimistic convention conceals every one of these effects. The uncertainty machinery we use is standard. The contribution is its evidence-based application, the pre-registered evaluation, and the finding that standard tools break down precisely where hardware operates.

</details>


### [102] [Efficient Estimation of Logical Sensitivities Through Fault-Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10531)
*Winston Fu,J. Wilson Staples,Jeff D. Thompson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可微估计器方法,能够从单次蒙特卡洛模拟数据集中同时获取量子纠错码逻辑错误率对所有物理噪声参数的灵敏度,相比传统有限差分法减少一到两个数量级的采样次数,并应用于误差预算量化、阈值轮廓追踪等量子纠错电路分析任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错电路受到多种物理噪声机制的影响,需要理解每种噪声对逻辑失败的贡献以评估编码性能和指导硬件改进。传统的逻辑灵敏度测量方法(如有限差分法)需要在多个噪声配置下分别测量逻辑错误率,效率低下且需要大量采样。因此,需要开发更高效的方法来同时获取所有噪声参数的灵敏度信息。

Method: 开发了一种可微估计器(differentiable estimator),利用底层故障配置信息,从单个噪声配置下的单次蒙特卡洛数据集中同时计算逻辑错误率对所有物理噪声参数的梯度∇_p p_L。该方法在表面码(surface code)的电路级噪声模拟中实现,并将灵敏度信息结合牛顿求根法用于定位和追踪多维噪声模型中的阈值轮廓。

Result: 在表面码电路级噪声模拟中,可微估计器与传统有限差分法结果一致,但仅需1-2个数量级更少的采样次数即可达到相同方差。该技术成功应用于量化误差预算、解析单个量子比特级别的灵敏度、推断有效码距,以及在多维噪声模型中定位和追踪阈值轮廓。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一种高效方法,可以从标准量子纠错模拟中提取和应用逻辑灵敏度信息。该可微估计器显著降低了灵敏度分析的计算成本,为量子纠错码的误差预算分析、阈值研究和硬件优化提供了实用工具,有助于更精确地理解不同物理噪声源对逻辑错误的贡献并指导量子计算硬件的改进方向。

Abstract: Quantum error-correcting circuits are affected by multiple physical noise mechanisms, whose contributions to logical failure must be understood to evaluate code performance and guide improvements in hardware. The individual error budget contributions for an error type $i$ can be characterized by its logical sensitivity $ν_i = \frac{\partial p_L}{\partial p_i}$, which measures the response of the logical error rate $p_L$ to each physical noise parameter $p_i$. Normally, $ν_i$ is measured using linear fits such as finite differences, where $p_L$ is measured at two or more values of $p_i$ to calculate partial derivatives. In this paper, we develop a differentiable estimator to obtain all components of $\nabla_{\mathbf p}p_L$ simultaneously from a single Monte Carlo data set at one noise configuration, using information about the underlying fault configurations. In surface code simulations with circuit-level noise, the estimator agrees with conventional finite differences while requiring one to two orders of magnitude fewer shots to achieve the same variance. We apply this technique to quantify error budgets, resolve sensitivities at the individual qubit level, and infer effective code distance. Finally, we incorporate the sensitivities into Newton root finding to locate and trace threshold contours in multidimensional noise models. These results provide an efficient method for extracting and applying logical sensitivity information from standard quantum error correction simulations.

</details>
